Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 1.25.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">5</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 30 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 11.60.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 10.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 16.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 0.83.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 2.90.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">20</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 120 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 2.90.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 9.67.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">10</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 60 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 5.80.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">15</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 90 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 3.87.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">5</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 30 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 11.60.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">8</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 48 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 7.25.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">10</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 60 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 1.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">15</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 90 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 0.67.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 2.90.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 10.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 20.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 72 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 4.83.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 2.90.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">8</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 48 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 16.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 12.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect

The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 7.25.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct

Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll. border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">6</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 36 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 9.67.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect

border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">5</td></tr></table><p>You roll a six-sided die 30 times. The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; color: #202020; background: #ffffff;"><tr style="background: #e3e8ed;"><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Degrees of freedom</th><th scope="col" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">Critical value at &alpha; = 0.05</th></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">4</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">9.49</td></tr><tr style="background: #f2f5f7;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">5</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">11.07</td></tr><tr style="background: #ffffff;"><th scope="row" style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right; font-weight: normal;">6</th><td style="padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #b8b8b8; text-align: right;">12.59</td></tr></table><p>Calculated &chi;<sup>2</sup> = 2.00.</p><p>Which one of the following conclusions is supported at &alpha; = 0.05?</p>	The null hypothesis is proven; every face has exactly the same chance on each roll.	Incorrect	Reject the null hypothesis; there is enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Incorrect	Do not reject the null hypothesis; there is not enough evidence that face probabilities differ from 1/6.	Correct	The null hypothesis is disproven; a fair die must give exactly equal counts of all faces.	Incorrect
